# Skills · N · part 42

859 entries, niagara-authoring to night-shift. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **niagara-authoring**: Use this skill when creating, inspecting, or modifying Niagara visual effects (`UNiagaraSystem`, `UNiagaraEmitter`, modules, renderers, user parameters, and level components) in Unreal Engine 5.9 using Epic's native `NiagaraToolsets` plugin (`NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Assets`, `NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_System`, `NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Component`, `NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Blueprint`, `NiagaraToolsets.NiagaraToolset_Info`). ([JanVogelsang/UE-AgentFramework](https://github.com/JanVogelsang/UE-AgentFramework/tree/HEAD/UnrealEngine/skills/niagara-authoring) · ★1)
- **niagara-emitters**: Niagara emitter color/curve authoring (tint, hue-shift, ColorFromCurve keys), rapid-iteration parameter tuning, and Custom-HLSL scratch-pad authoring (NiagaraEmitterService + NiagaraScratchPadService). Module/renderer CRUD is owned by the engine NiagaraToolsets. Use when the user asks to recolor or hue-shift particles, edit color curves, tune emitter params, or build a scratch-pad/Custom HLSL module. For system-level lifecycle, load niagara-systems. ([kevinpbuckley/VibeUE](https://github.com/kevinpbuckley/VibeUE/tree/HEAD/Content/Skills/niagara-emitters) · ★842 · 8 copies)
- **niagara-emitters**: Configure Niagara emitter internals — modules, renderers, rapid-iteration parameters, color/curve editing, graph positioning, and Custom-HLSL scratch-pad authoring. Use when the user asks to add modules/renderers to a Niagara emitter, recolor or hue-shift particles, set emitter parameters, build an emitter from scratch, or author a scratch-pad/Custom HLSL module. For system-level lifecycle, load niagara-systems. ([MJL1106/ProjectExtract](https://github.com/MJL1106/ProjectExtract/tree/HEAD/Extraction/Plugins/VibeUE/Content/Skills/niagara-emitters))
- **niagara-explosion-sprite-sheets-godot**: Make Godot explosions look like Unreal Niagara: build the explosion in UE Niagara, render it out to sprite sheets with depth and thermal data baked in, and import the sheets into Godot, where they play at zero frame cost. Trigger when a Godot game needs one hero explosion effect that the engine's built-in particles cannot reach. ([Emperiusm/skill_library](https://github.com/Emperiusm/skill_library/tree/HEAD/skills/simulation-vfx/niagara-explosion-sprite-sheets-godot) · ★1)
- **niagara-fluids**: Realistic fire, smoke and explosions with Unreal's Niagara Fluids (real-time simulation), tuned through the Niagara editor with the Slate inspector, and baked into flipbook textures so fast particles carry real fire. Use when the owner wants an effect to look real (not cartoon), when tuning a Grid3D gas/fire system, or when making a flipbook from a simulation. ([Waffle-Widgets/jump-cut](https://github.com/Waffle-Widgets/jump-cut/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/niagara-fluids) · 2 copies)
- **niagara-fx**: Make attack and impact effects with real Niagara particles through the Unreal MCP (beams, trails, sparks, flares, fades), judge them from the game camera and tune them with the owner. Use when an effect looks flat or weak, or the user asks for particles, a new attack, a hit effect or a fade-out. ([Waffle-Widgets/jump-cut](https://github.com/Waffle-Widgets/jump-cut/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/niagara-fx) · 2 copies)
- **niagara-graph**: Draw an Unreal Niagara script graph as a real node graph on the web instead of describing it in terminal text. Use whenever explaining a Niagara scratch pad, module script, dynamic input or function script of three or more connected nodes - Map Get/Map Set chains, operator maths on particle attributes, Custom HLSL - or when the user asks to see a Niagara graph, wants a node setup "as a picture", or asks which pins to add to a Map Get. Emits Niagara clipboard T3D, so the same output pastes straight into a script graph with Ctrl+V. Also builds multi-section build-guide pages, where the emitter… ([HaJH/unreal-graph-skills](https://github.com/HaJH/unreal-graph-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/niagara-graph) · ★1)
- **niagara-platform**: Entry point: operate a Niagara 4 host (stations, modules, daemon, logs) through its own command-line tools with allowlists, confirmation gates and an audit trail. Use when the user asks to list/start/stop/restart stations, install modules, read station logs, check host health, or manage the Niagara service. ([S-Tier-Building-Automation/niagara-agent-skills](https://github.com/S-Tier-Building-Automation/niagara-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/niagara-platform))

- **niagara-systems**: Rapid-iteration parameter tuning, diagnostics, and Custom-HLSL scratch-pad authoring for Niagara systems (VibeUE NiagaraService + NiagaraScratchPadService). Use when the user asks to tune emitter rapid-iteration params, compare/diagnose systems, or build scratch-pad/Custom HLSL modules. For emitter color/module work, load niagara-emitters. - **niagara-systems**: Create and manage Niagara particle systems — system lifecycle, add/copy emitters, user parameters, and system script settings (NiagaraService). Use when the user asks to create a Niagara system, add or copy emitters into a system, add user/exposed parameters, or compile a system. For emitter internals, load niagara-emitters. - **niagara-vfx**: Create and control visual effects using Unreal's Niagara system — UNiagaraSystem, UNiagaraComponent, UNiagaraFunctionLibrary; the system/emitter/module/parameter hierarchy; spawning effects at a world location or attached to an actor/socket (SpawnSystemAtLocation, SpawnSystemAttached); setting User Parameters from C++ (SetVariableFloat, SetVariableVec3, SetVariableActor, SetFloatParameter, SetColorParameter); CPU vs GPU simulation trade-offs; and Data Interfaces (skeletal mesh, static mesh, collision). Use when spawning particle or VFX (fire, smoke, impacts, trails, magic), attaching effects… ([Kright/unreal-engine-vite-skills](https://github.com/Kright/unreal-engine-vite-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/core/niagara-vfx) · ★1)
- **niagara-vfx**: Niagara is Unreal's visual-effects system. It replaced **Cascade** (legacy — avoid for new work; a converter plugin exists). Effects are authored in the Niagara Editor as **Systems** composed of **Emitters** built from **Modules**, then spawned and parameterized from gameplay C++. Your role in C++ is: spawn a system, attach it, set its User Parameters, and manage its lifetime. ([ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game](https://github.com/ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/niagara-vfx) · ★1)
- **niah-bench**: Needle-in-a-Haystack benchmark setup and customization for Strix Halo. Covers classic NIAH, NoLiMa (reasoning-based), and replacing synthetic filler with real codebases. Use when testing long-context retrieval quality. ([fabiantax/claude-plugins](https://github.com/fabiantax/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ml/skills/niah-bench))
- **niaid-bp-api-assess**: Assess an OpenAPI/Swagger document or an API documentation page against NIAID Blueprint Section 3 (JSON-LD metadata, resource IRIs, HTTP GET, OpenAPI) and a short REST lint. Writes findings JSON and a gap report. Use when the user supplies a swagger file, an OpenAPI URL, or API docs and wants to know how well the API is expressed, or says "assess this API", "review our OpenAPI", "Blueprint API alignment", or /niaid-bp-api-assess. ([go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core](https://github.com/go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core/tree/HEAD/niaid-blueprint/skills/niaid-bp-api-assess) · ★2)
- **niaid-bp-citation**: Guide users through generating citation text and BibTeX entries aligned with the NIAID Blueprint for Digital Objects (Section 4). Supports original-data deposits, reused-data attribution, and repository-level citations with PID integration (DOI, RRID, ORCID). Use when the user wants citation examples, BibTeX for a dataset or software object, a "How to Cite" page draft, or runs /niaid-bp-citation. ([go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core](https://github.com/go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core/tree/HEAD/niaid-blueprint/skills/niaid-bp-citation) · ★2)
- **niaid-bp-dataset-intake**: Conducts a conversational interview to collect metadata for a schema.org Dataset and produces a valid JSON-LD document aligned with the NIAID Blueprint for Digital Objects. Covers all 16 Blueprint metadata elements across five natural groups: identity, provenance, content, access, and context. Use when a researcher or repository manager needs structured dataset metadata, or says "create metadata for my dataset", "generate JSON-LD", "describe my dataset", or "Blueprint metadata". ([go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core](https://github.com/go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core/tree/HEAD/niaid-blueprint/skills/niaid-bp-dataset-intake) · ★2)
- **niaid-bp-fair-assess**: Conducts a structured NIAID Blueprint FAIR assessment interview across six phases (resource overview, metadata schema, persistent identifiers, APIs, citation, and outreach/training) and produces a prioritized gap report with actionable next steps. Use when a repository owner, data steward, or PI wants to evaluate Blueprint or FAIR alignment, prepare for NIAID Data Ecosystem Discovery Portal integration, or says "assess my repository", "Blueprint assessment", "FAIR gap analysis", or "how do we integrate with the NIAID Portal". ([go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core](https://github.com/go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core/tree/HEAD/niaid-blueprint/skills/niaid-bp-fair-assess) · ★2)
- **niaid-bp-llms**: Check that a site publishes an llms.txt file, that the Markdown follows the llms.txt v2 spec (H1, optional blockquote, H2 file lists), and that a sample of its same-host links answer. Use when the user gives a repository or site URL and asks about llms.txt, or runs /niaid-bp-llms. Produces a JSON-LD record plus metadata notes — no user interview required. Use when the user provides a dataset or resource URL for automatic extraction, or says "/niaid-bp-metadata-extract", "extract metadata from URL", "generate JSON-LD from this page", "Blueprint metadata from URL", or "scrape metadata for this resource". ([go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core](https://github.com/go-fair-us/ai-blueprint-core/tree/HEAD/niaid-blueprint/skills/niaid-bp-metadata-extract) · ★2)

- **nianxiang-content-os**: 念响内容OS（完整名称：念响OS · AI内容获客系统），统一处理素材讨论、选题、个人IP朋友圈与短视频口播的创作、改稿和审稿，以及按授权的作品归档、内容范式与知识沉淀。称呼“阿响”，或说“挖掘选题”“写朋友圈”“做口播”“补朋友圈沉淀”及表达对应意图时使用；设置、调整、暂停、恢复定时任务，或安装念响、初始化私人目录、更换运行环境时也使用。专项方法在包内按需读取，不另装配套Skill；无私人知识库也能用所给素材创作。整套宣传资产、客户宣传片和分镜任务按环境发现外部专项能力。 ([KaenZhong/nianxiang-content-os](https://github.com/KaenZhong/nianxiang-content-os/tree/HEAD/nianxiang-content-os) · ★4)
